model TimetableProject {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdById String
  createdBy   User   @relation("TimetableProjectCreator", fields: [createdById], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)

  name         String
  academicYear String
  semester     String

  status TimetableProjectStatus @default(DRAFT)

  daysJson     Json
  periodsJson  Json
  settingsJson Json?

  teachers          TimetableTeacher[]
  classes           TimetableClass[]
  subjects          TimetableSubject[]
  classSubjects     TimetableClassSubject[]
  assignments       TimetableAssignment[]
  waitingPolicy     TimetableWaitingPolicy?
  dailyAbsences     TimetableDailyAbsence[]
  substitutions     TimetableSubstitution[]
  supervisionDuties TimetableSupervisionDuty[]
  constraints       TimetableConstraint[]

  schedules TimetableSchedule[]
  createdAt DateTime            @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime            @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([createdById])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([createdAt])
}

  placementScore Int @default(0)

  metadataJson Json?

  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([scheduleId, classId, dayId, periodId], map: "TSE_schedule_class_slot_key")
  @@unique([scheduleId, teacherId, dayId, periodId], map: "TSE_schedule_teacher_slot_key")
  @@index([scheduleId], map: "TSE_schedule_idx")
  @@index([teacherId, dayId, periodId], map: "TSE_teacher_slot_idx")
  @@index([classId, dayId, periodId], map: "TSE_class_slot_idx")
  @@index([subjectId], map: "TSE_subject_idx")
  @@index([assignmentId], map: "TSE_assignment_idx")
